### ⭐⭐ `ADR-0134` — The `PRD-008` payment divergence: **successor path DETERMINED, frozen text PRESERVED, divergence DISCLOSED** (2026-09-10)

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **ADR** | [`ADR-0134`](./ADR-0134-prd-008-payment-divergence-successor-path-determined-frozen-text-preserved.md) |
| **Status** | **`Accepted`** — 2026-09-10, by direct, explicit conferral of the human principal, jointly exercising **Product Owner**, **Architecture Owner** and **Governance Owner** authority in one act. ⛔ All offices **revert on completion** (`ADR-0033` §7.1) |
| **Act** | ⭐ **Audit the frozen `PRD-008` payment conflict against `ADR-0130` and determine the correct governed successor/amendment path.** ⛔ **NOTHING ELSE** |
| **⭐⭐ Central outcome** | ⭐ **The route is DETERMINED and deliberately NOT EXECUTED.** The lawful instrument is a **`PRD-008` v1.8 successor amendment** under `PRD_LIFECYCLE` §4's seven-step frozen-PRD route, bounded by **`S-1`…`S-8`**. ⛔⛔ **`PRD-008` is BYTE-UNCHANGED** — verified by blob hash before and after |
| **The exact conflict** | FROZEN `PRD-008` v1.7 §6.1 lists **L209** UPI · **L210** card · **L211** online · **L213** payment verification · **L214** webhook reconciliation as **V1**, contradicting `ADR-0130` §1.1. ⭐ **L208 `Cash payment` is CORRECT at V1** and is expressly protected by **`S-1`**. ⭐ Re-verified line by line against the live file, ⛔ **not copied from `ADR-0130` on trust** |
| **⭐⭐ Governing precedence** | `DOCUMENTATION_BASELINE` **§4** — **Rank 2** Accepted ADRs outrank **Rank 3** module PRDs — so `ADR-0130` governs **today, with no edit**, and the five rows are **superseded in effect**. ⛔ **`ADR-0130` is NOT re-decided, reopened or weakened** |
| **⚠⚠ The real finding** | ⭐⭐ **PRECEDENCE IS NOT DISCOVERABILITY, and that was the whole of `B-4`'s residual risk.** Precedence says *who wins* only once a reader knows both documents exist; it does nothing for a reader who opens a **FROZEN, baseline-authoritative** PRD and never learns `ADR-0130` exists |
| **⭐ What was already protected — and NOT duplicated** | `ADR-0131` **§9** / `PRD_LIFECYCLE` **§7A.9 L403** already bind **every** Technical Specification with the PAYMENT clause and already **name this trap with all five line numbers**. ⛔ **Re-legislating it would be duplicate governance**, so this act adds nothing there |
| **⭐⭐ The measured gap this act closes** | **G1** `DOCUMENTATION_BASELINE` **L175** — the Rank-authoritative `PRD-008` row — contained **ZERO** mentions of `ADR-0130`/cash-only/UPI/V2 and read simply *"Authoritative"* · **G2** `PRD_REGISTRY.md` contained **ZERO** `ADR-0130` references across **1,777** lines. ⭐ **Exactly the two entry points a specification author consults, and neither carried a warning** |
| **⛔⛔ Three instruments tested and REJECTED** | **(a)** editing the five rows now — ⛔ refused on `PRD_LIFECYCLE` **L177** (*"A frozen PRD is never silently modified. Not for an obvious correction, and not for one that is certainly right"*), the rule that anticipates this exact temptation, with **L178**'s `PGA-01`/`PGA-02` precedent of **confirmed** defects left standing · **(b)** a **v2 re-baseline** — ⛔ disproportionate: **5 of 249** items against re-opening **134 obligations / 98 AC / 10 invariants / 17 open `FEE-GAP-*` / 83 `IMPL-*` traces** · **(c)** a **new PRD identity** — ⛔ barred by **L67**, since `PRD-008` already owns `BC-05` |
| **⭐⭐⭐ Why this is NOT §4 step 1's ADR** | ⚠ Deciding **what the amended text says** — which rows, what release value, the effect on `FEE-FR-028` and `FEE-AC-085`…`089` — requires the **`BC-05` Domain Owner** (`PRD_OWNERSHIP_MODEL` **L84**), an office ⛔ **not conferred**. ⭐ So the act determines the **route** (a Governance act about process) and **stops**; deciding **content** is a Domain + Product act about requirements. ⚠ **The absence of a v1.8 is the CORRECT outcome, not incomplete work** |
| **The successor's bounded mandate** | **`S-1`** only L209/L210/L211/L213/L214, ⛔ **never L208** · **`S-2`** prior text **verbatim** (**L207**) · **`S-3`** cite `ADR-0130`, don't re-decide it · **`S-4`** close no `FEE-GAP-*` · **`S-5`** preserve **`MP-GBR-24`** · **`S-6`** ⛔ **invent no V1 digital verification/gateway/webhook** · **`S-7`** ⛔ **delete no obligation to win a scope argument** · **`S-8`** one commit |
| **⚠ Residues disclosed and ROUTED, not repaired** | `PRD-019` (**39** Razorpay refs, **0** `ADR-0130`) — `ADR-0130` §10 item 8 · EA **L1395**/**L1397**/**L1398** still *(V1)* — item 9, ⭐ **lowest risk: Rank 6 descriptive, loses by rank automatically** · BC Map **L202**'s UPI example — item 10, ⭐ **boundary correct and unaffected**. ⛔ **All three stay OPEN** |
| **⚠ A drafting error caught before commit** | ⭐ An earlier draft attributed baseline **L175**'s citation to `PRD-015_STAGE3_CLOSURE_ASSESSMENT.md` **L56** — ⛔ **wrong**: L56 cites **`PRD_REGISTRY.md` L175**, a different file. The true citer is **`ADR-0130` L186**, an **Accepted** ADR — which raised the bar: the amendment had to **preserve the quoted phrase *"Authoritative — the Revenue & Finance baseline"* VERBATIM**, not merely stay on the same line. ⭐ Recorded in §6.1, ⛔ not silently fixed |
| **Files amended** | **3, each minimally:** `DOCUMENTATION_BASELINE.md` (**L175** in place + §1 Authority + §8 row) · `PRD_REGISTRY.md` (**new §20 appended at END OF FILE**, **0** of its citations shifted) · `ADR-INDEX.md` (this entry + `Count`) |
| **⚠ Baseline identifier UNCHANGED** | ⭐ §7 **rule 4**'s limb is **UNMET** — ⛔ **no Rank 1–3 document changes version**: `PRD-008` stays **v1.7 byte-unchanged**, `MASTER_PRD` **v1.10**, BC Map **v1.15**; the amended files are the **unranked** declaration, registry and index. **`BASELINE-2026-09-10-C` STANDS** — ⭐ itself evidence the act is correctly scoped, since a determination about a **route** should not move the baseline. ⚠ The future v1.8 **will** move it |
| **`B-4` disposition** | ⭐ **DISPOSED — by determination and disclosure.** ⛔⛔ **NOT "closed by correction":** §6.1 still reads `V1` on five rows and will until a `BC-05` Domain Owner executes v1.8. ⭐ **An honest disposition names the remaining divergence rather than implying the text is fixed** |

### ⭐⭐ `ADR-0135` — API governance: style, error taxonomy and non-disclosing denial, versioning, idempotency transport, session boundary, authorization alignment (2026-09-10)

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **ADR** | [`ADR-0135`](./ADR-0135-api-governance-style-error-taxonomy-versioning-idempotency-transport.md) |
| **Status** | **`Accepted`** — 2026-09-10, by direct, explicit conferral of the human principal, exercising **Architecture Owner** authority — the office `ADR-0131` §12 names for **`B-5`**. ⛔ The office **reverts on completion** (`ADR-0033` §7.1) |
| **Act** | ⭐ **Govern the six `B-5` API decisions** — style, error taxonomy + non-disclosing denial, versioning, idempotency-key transport, session/token boundary, authorization alignment. ⛔ **NOTHING ELSE** |
| **⭐⭐ Central outcome** | ⭐ **`API-1`…`API-9` DECIDED; `API-GAP-001` STOPPED and recorded OPEN.** ⛔⛔ **ZERO permission identifiers, ZERO endpoints, ZERO OpenAPI, ZERO Technical Specifications** |
| **⭐⭐⭐ Four of six were ALREADY decided at higher rank — recognised, not re-decided** | **Style** ← Rank-1 `MASTER_PRD` **L407** (*"REST API conventions (and GraphQL where the BFF requires it)"*) + Rank-4 BC Map **L226** + `MP-CON-09` · **Non-disclosing denial** ← ⭐ **`Accepted` `ADR-0010` L151**, which had **already rejected** *"Distinct 404/403 for private vs non-existent"* as an *"**enumeration oracle**"*, plus FROZEN `AUTH-10.33`, Rank-3 `LCM-API-003`, byte-frozen `AP-9` · **Idempotency** ← ⭐ **FROZEN `FEE-FR-029`** (*"MUST accept an idempotency key **at the API edge**"*) + `FEE-INV-005` + Rank-1 `MP-GBR-18` · **Session boundary** ← Rank-1 `MP-GBR-26` + Rank-4 Matrix **L220** (*"ask `BC-18`, **never evaluate or cache**"*) |
| **`API-1`/`API-2`/`API-3` — style** | REST over HTTP for the V1 external surface · GraphQL **only** at the BFF/OHS edge **where the BFF requires it** (⛔ not a general style, ⛔ not a second surface; GraphQL/gRPC measured **0** occurrences beyond the two governing phrases) · cross-context calls are **in-process port calls**, ⛔ **never internal HTTP** — under `MP-CON-09` an internal HTTP hop is a **boundary violation**, not a style choice |
| **`API-4`/`API-5`/`API-6` — denial** | ⛔⛔ A denial **MUST NOT disclose existence** — *"exists but forbidden"* and *"does not exist"* **MUST be indistinguishable**; ⛔ distinct 404/403 for private-vs-absent **PROHIBITED** · ⛔ **MUST NOT disclose the reason** (failing rule, conjunct, capability, role, policy, tenant, another principal's state) · ⭐ **the asymmetry is the rule**: validation errors **MAY** be specific (`MASTER_PRD` **L410**) because they concern the *request*; authorization/existence outcomes **MUST NOT** be, because they concern the *world*. ⭐ An `AP-9` **fail-closed** refusal **MUST look identical** to an entitlement refusal |
| **⚠⚠ `API-7`/`API-8` — the one decision needing real judgement** | Rank 1 **L408** requires *"**Versioned endpoints**"* while **Rank 6** EA **L380**/**L1787** date versioning **(V2)**. ⭐ Resolved **by rank** (§4; baseline **L139** — EA is *"**Descriptive** — must follow the PRDs, never lead them"*) **and by distinguishing an interface *property* from management *machinery***: a V1 surface can carry a version without running a deprecation programme. ⭐ **`API-7`** the surface **MUST** carry a version identifier from first release; ⚠ **`API-8`** the **mechanism is NOT selected** — path/header/media-type/query all **open**, because choosing one needs a concrete surface that ⛔ **does not exist** |
| **⛔⛔ `API-GAP-001` — THE STOP** | The **deprecation / sunset / multi-version-support policy** is **UNDECIDED**. Measured: `grep "deprecation window\|sunset period\|support window"` → **0** across all of `docs/`. ⭐ It is a **Product** commitment about what is owed to consumers, not an Architecture property of the interface, and **Product Owner authority was not conferred** — so it is **recorded, not guessed**. ⭐ **Why it is safe to leave open:** V1 has **no external API consumer** — `Public Developer APIs` is **(Future)** at EA **L1783** and no partner/public programme exists, so with one first-party client a version retires by shipping the client |
| **`API-9` — idempotency transport** | An idempotency key is **request METADATA, not domain payload** — caller-supplied, **opaque to the domain**, unique **per tenant** (as `FEE-INV-005` already scopes it; `AP-6` makes that inevitable). ⭐ **Recognition, not invention:** `FEE-FR-029` places it *"at the **API edge**"*, not in an aggregate, and `MP-GBR-18` treats it as the third element of a **replay-discriminator tuple**. ⛔ **No header name, format, length, retention or collision status code is specified** |
| **Session / token boundary (narrow by design)** | ⭐ The API edge **consumes** authorization decisions via `identity.policy_decision` and ⛔ **MUST NOT evaluate, cache, or infer** one from a token — `MP-GBR-26`'s *"no propagation window"* makes a cached decision **unsound by construction**. ⛔ **A token is an authentication artefact, NOT an authorization store.** ⛔ `AUTH-3.12` restated, not extended. ⭐ Token format/lifetime/refresh/rotation stay **Auth v3.0 / `ADR-0129`** — ⭐ **another office's settled scope, not a gap**; ⛔ no Auth v4.0 |
| **Authorization alignment — a prohibition, not a creation** | ⛔⛔ **ZERO permission identifiers created**; `AUTH-7.22` **CLOSED at 0**; `ADR-0043` §5.1 binding; ⛔ **`ADR-0132` NOT reopened**. Authorization is **role + action class + scope class evaluated as policy** (`AP-8`) over the closed vocabularies. Derived capability (`LCM-AUTH-001`/`002`) is the sanctioned route. ⛔⛔ **Endpoint-keyed grants and route allow-lists PROHIBITED** — ⭐ each would build a permission vocabulary **outside** the closed catalogue. ⚠ The **`AP-4`/`AP-8` tension** is neither resolved nor relied on |
| **⚠⚠ Namespace measured, and the obvious choice REFUSED** | Bare `API-NNN` measured **free (0)** — yet ⛔ three-digit `API-001` was **NOT minted**, because **400** `LCF-`/`LCR-`/`LCM-`/`MSG-`/`SGR-`/`SSF-`/`SDS-`/`LCO-API-NNN` identifiers exist in baselined Rank-3 `PRD-021A`/`B`, and a bare twin would collide in `grep`, in traceability and in the reader's eye. ⭐ Single-digit **`API-1`…`API-9`** matches this repository's architecture-rule convention (`AP-3`…`AP-9`, `AR-1`…`AR-7`, `ID-1`…`ID-6`, `CID-1`…`CID-6`). ⛔ **`AERR-1`…`AERR-13`** (Authentication v2, 14 distinct) **not reused or extended**. ⭐ These are **ADR-local architecture rules** — ⛔ not PRD requirements, ⛔ not traceability entries, ⛔ not `IMPL-*` |
| **Files amended** | **3, each minimally:** `PRD_LIFECYCLE.md` (**v1.1 → v1.2**; ⭐ **ONE new `API` row appended to §7A.9**, which carried four domains and ⛔ **no API domain** — ⭐ that absence *was* the structural gap) · `ADR-INDEX.md` (this entry + `Count`) · `DOCUMENTATION_BASELINE.md` (§1 Authority + §8 row) |

| **⚠ A pre-existing defect disclosed, NOT repaired** | `PRD_LIFECYCLE` §10's changelog separator sits at **L453**, *below* the v1.1 data row at **L452** instead of under the header — ⭐ **a malformation introduced by the v1.1 act itself** (`024e20d`, my own `B-1` commit). The v1.2 row is inserted **directly after the separator** (lawful Markdown, newest-first); ⛔ **re-ordering L452 is NOT done** — unrelated cosmetic drift, and tidying a changelog row is the silent edit **L177**'s discipline warns against |

### ⭐⭐ `ADR-0129` — Google Sign-In is the V1 primary authentication method; Mobile OTP is re-scoped to V2 (2026-09-10) — ⚠ **REGISTERED LATE, 2026-09-11**

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **ADR** | [`ADR-0129`](./ADR-0129-google-sign-in-v1-primary-authentication-mobile-otp-v2.md) |
| **Status** | ⭐ **`Accepted`** — 2026-09-10. Promoted `Proposed` → `Accepted` in a **separate governed acceptance act**, with **no substantive change**: only the status cell moved; the decision text is **byte-unchanged** |
| **Deciders** | ⭐⭐ **Architecture Owner + Product Owner, JOINTLY, with Privacy Owner sign-off** — three offices for three limbs: scope/priority (Product, `PRD_OWNERSHIP_MODEL` **L83**), boundaries/ranks (Architecture, **L85**), minor-inclusivity/consent (Privacy) |
| **Decision** | ⭐⭐ **Google Sign-In (Google OIDC) is the V1 primary authentication method and the SOLE required one; Mobile OTP is RE-SCOPED to V2.** The immutable Google **`sub`** is the provider identity key; **`AccountId`** is the canonical internal identity, permanent and non-reusable; **`PersonId`** remains `BC-10`-owned and atomic (`ADR-0011`); ⛔ no password, ⛔ no email-as-identity, ⛔ no V1 email recovery, ⛔ no demo/guest/seeded sign-in |
| **Governs** | `BC-18` Identity & Access |
| **Supersedes** | ⚠ **`ADR-0002`** (Mobile OTP as the sole V1 factor) and ⚠ **`ADR-0005`** (Account creation on first OTP) — **REQUIRED, NOT YET EXECUTED** (`ADR-0129` §7). ⛔ Both remain `Accepted` in this register until a governed supersession act runs |
| **Baseline effect** | ⭐ **Authentication PRD v3.0** is the authoritative `BC-18` specification — `DOCUMENTATION_BASELINE` **L258**; v2.0 is **superseded, historical, frozen** |
| **⚠⚠ Why this entry is dated later than the ADR** | ⭐⭐ **A REGISTRATION OMISSION, DISCLOSED FOUR TIMES AND NOW REPAIRED.** `ADR-0129` was written, accepted and relied upon from 2026-09-10, but was **never registered here**; the `Count` cell recorded the omission under `ADR-0130`, `ADR-0131`, `ADR-0132` and `ADR-0133` without any pass authoring the entry, each correctly holding it outside its own conferral. ⭐ Registered now under the **`ADR-0057` late-registration discipline** (this file **L8**), as Stage-1 audit item **`SX-04`** |
| **⚠ A correction to the audit's own wording** | The Stage-1 audit called the defect a missing *"**main-table** register row."* ⭐ Re-measurement shows that framing was **wrong**: `ADR-0128` has **0** main-table rows too — the `Register` table ends at **`ADR-0114`**, and ⭐⭐ **`ADR-0115` … `ADR-0135` are ALL registered as end-of-file addendum sections.** This entry therefore follows its **eleven neighbours**; ⛔ a main-table row would have been inconsistent **and** would have shifted **9** live citations |
| **⛔ What this registration does NOT do** | ⛔ **`ADR-0129` is byte-unchanged** — not edited, not re-decided, not re-statused, ⛔ **not reopened** · ⛔ **no duplicate entry** (measured: **0** prior register rows and **0** prior addendum sections) · ⛔ **`ADR-0002`/`ADR-0005` NOT superseded here** — that act is still owed · ⛔ no baseline, version or freeze changed |

## `ADR-0144` — `PRD-021A` **Part A8** REMAINS in its current form; the **A8 limb** of `TSG-GAP-003` is DISPOSED; the gap is **CLOSED**

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Status** | ⭐ **Accepted** — 2026-09-11 |
| **File** | [`ADR-0144-prd-021a-a8-remains-in-current-form-tsg-gap-003-a8-limb-disposed-gap-closed.md`](./ADR-0144-prd-021a-a8-remains-in-current-form-tsg-gap-003-a8-limb-disposed-gap-closed.md) |
| **Authority** | ⭐ **Architecture Owner + Product Owner**, by direct, explicit conferral of the human principal — ⭐ **the exact pairing `ADR-0131` §11 names for `TSG-GAP-003`** — ⛔ **reverting on completion** (`ADR-0033` §7.1). ⚠ **Both offices exercised by one principal**; ⛔ **no independent review, ARB quorum, Security review or Legal opinion is claimed** |
| **⭐⭐ Determination `D-1`** | ⭐⭐ **OUTCOME B — A8 SHALL REMAIN IN ITS CURRENT FORM.** The A8 limb is **DISPOSED IN THE NEGATIVE**: A8 ⛔ **is NOT re-expressed, converted, rewritten, renamed, re-identified, restatused or moved** into a `TS-*` document; ⭐ it **remains a Rank-3 part** at its own identifier and version (`Accepted` `ADR-0087`), available as ⭐ **cited input and structural precedent** (`ADR-0131` §6.1). ⭐ **Limited to A8** |
| **⭐⭐⭐ Determination `D-2`** | ⭐⭐⭐ **`TSG-GAP-003` IS CLOSED.** All three limbs the gap names are disposed by the required authority — **B9** (`ADR-0140` `D-2`), **C8** (`ADR-0142` `D-2`), **A8** (`D-1`) — and the gap names ⛔ **no fourth subject**. ⚠ **Closure was TESTED against the gap's own wording, not asserted because this ADR exists**; ⚠ it means **only** that the re-expression question is answered — ⛔ **all three answers were NEGATIVE** |
| **⭐⭐ Why this is a genuine negative, not a default** | ⭐ A8's entry gates were measured and ⭐ **both PASS** — **`E-1`** by `Accepted` **`ADR-0087`** (A1–A8 at **Rank 3**, A8 named 11×) and **`E-2`** by the Stage-3 records determining *"A8 ✅ **STAGE 3 CLEARED — 6/6**"* and *"`PRD-021A` is **Stage-3 complete across A1–A8**"* — so ⛔ **the easy disqualification was looked for and NOT found**. ⛔⛔ **Both cheap arguments rejected by name:** ⛔ *"choose A because `TS-001`/`TS-002` exist"* (⭐⭐ **writing a `TS-*` about a part is the opposite of re-expressing the part as one** — both those limbs were disposed **negatively**) and ⛔ *"choose B to avoid the work"* (⛔ effort is not a governance reason) |
| **⭐ The seven grounds** | **(1)** two prior limbs disposed negatively by the same authority, ⛔ **no distinguishing feature found**; **(2)** re-expression would **strand citations** across the `PRD-021A` Stage-3/4/5/6/7 records, `ADR-0087`, `ADR-0083` and `ADR-0131` §5.3/§6.1 — ⭐ *"An ADR supersedes; **it does not delete**"* (**L207**); **(3)** ⭐⭐ **it would DEMOTE a Rank-3 baselined record into an UNRANKED one** (§7A.7, `ADR-0131` §8.3) with **`TSG-GAP-004` still OPEN** so nothing could restore it — **a governance LOSS**; **(4)** ⛔⛔ **`P-5` bars a specification from doing the one thing A8 most needs**; **(5)** **≥9** open gaps, **1** unresolved conflict, **1** unwritten required ADR against C8's **0/0/0** — ⭐ **restating a gap register is duplication, not re-expression**; **(6)** A8 is **V2**, Stage 4 ⛔ **not entered**; **(7)** ⭐ §6.1 **already** permits citing A8 *"as input and as structural precedent"* — so conversion is **unnecessary** |
| **⛔⛔ `LCT-CONF-001` / `P-5`** | ⛔⛔ **EXPRESSLY NOT RESOLVED.** A8 §15.3 calls it *"the most material finding in A8"*; §41.3 marks it ⛔ **"RAISED, NOT RESOLVED"** → **`LCT-ADR-001`, UNWRITTEN**. Measured: A2's **`LCF-FR-104`** closes the event set at **six** (*"A seventh SHALL require a PRD amendment"*) against A7's **seven** — **1** exact match, **2** name divergences (one also **semantic**: *Added* vs *Changed*), **4** with **no counterpart**. ⭐⭐ **A `TS-*` could adopt neither set without resolving the conflict (`P-5`) or reaching A2's frozen requirement (`P-2`), and could not cite the non-existent `LCT-ADR-001` (`E-3`)** — ⭐ so **A8's existing treatment is already the treatment `P-5` prescribes**. ⛔ **A2's event set, `LCF-FR-104` and A7's register untouched**; ⛔ **no event named, renamed, added or removed**; ⛔ **no preference between six and seven expressed or implied**. **Owner: Social Domain owner**, Architecture Owner consulted on naming |
| **⛔ Decisions `D-3`/`D-4`** | ⛔⛔ **A8 BYTE-UNCHANGED** (`1a0fbcf39cc58bdb8e95750fcaf995e7ecdd579b`, **1,925 lines**, `DRAFT v0.1`, **Rank 3**); ⛔ **nothing in it is ratified as technically correct**. ⛔⛔ **NO `TS-003` created, authorised, allocated or reserved** (measured **0** files); ⛔ **no `TS-*` identifier, directory, stub, template, `README` or `.gitkeep`** — ⭐ Outcome **B** requires none |

## ⭐⭐ ADDENDUM — `ADR-0157` registration *(end-of-file; ⛔ L9 Count cell NOT edited)*

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **ADR** | ⭐⭐ **[`ADR-0157`](ADR-0157-seat-cfg-seat-management-configuration-authorization-allocation.md)** — *`SEAT-CFG-*`: `TR-1`-only tenant authorization for 15, with `005`/`014` and `017` HELD* |
| **Status** | ⭐⭐ **Accepted** — 2026-09-19 · **Rank 2** · `BC-25` *(owning)*, `BC-07` *(source)*, `BC-06` *(adjacent)* |
| **Authority** | ⭐⭐ **Four decisions, verbatim** — `SE-1` *(Product + SECURITY PLATFORM)* · `SE-2` *(Authorization Owner)* · `SE-3` *(Product + `BC-06` Owner)* · `SE-4` *(Architecture Owner)* |
| **⚠⚠ THE FIRST REGISTER THAT SPLITS FOUR WAYS** | ⭐ **A** routine tenant **15** · ⛔ **B** authorization-semantic **2** *(`005`, `014`)* · ⛔ **C** not `BC-25`-owned, value unset **1** *(`017`)* · ⚠ **D** scope caveat *(`SEAT-XC-022`)*. ⭐ **15 + 2 + 1 = 18**; ⛔ batching it as one would have been wrong on **three** counts |
| **⭐⭐ `SE-1` — `TR-2` EXCLUDED, and the exclusion was VERIFIED** | ⭐ **`SEAT-PO-021`** *(**L2005**, FROZEN)* reads **Owner Y · Manager — · Reception — · Student —**; **`SEAT-BR-039`**: *"Manager **MUST NOT** hold the tenant-shaping operations, which remain **Owner-only**: … **configuration (`SEAT-PO-021`)**"*. ⚠⚠ **A REAL DIVERGENCE FROM `ADR-0156`**, where `TR-2` held `WRITE` over `SMCFG-*` — ⭐ the difference is **sourced**: `PRD-004` had no Owner-only rule, `PRD-007` states one. ⛔ **The `SMCFG` allocation was NOT copied** |
| **⭐⭐ `READ` is NARROWER than elsewhere, and was decided SEPARATELY** | ⚠ `PRD-007` defines **no** configuration read operation *(`SEAT-PO-024` covers the seat map and metrics, ⛔ not the register)*. ⭐⭐ **`CNF-FR-081` prescribes how a read already held must be RENDERED — ⛔ it does not GRANT one.** ⭐ So **`READ` = `TR-1` only**, matching `WRITE`; ⛔ **`TR-2`/`TR-3` read is NOT allocated** — that would be derivation, barred by `AUTH-7.24` |
| **⛔⛔ `SE-2` — 2 HELD, authorization-semantic** | ⭐ The `SEAT-PO-*` legend defines **`C`** as *"permitted **only where the named configurable grants it**"* — ⭐ **a `C` cell is an authorization conditional, not a preference**. **`SEAT-CFG-005`** *("**which roles** beyond Owner may override…"; `SEAT-PO-010` = `C`)* and **`SEAT-CFG-014`** *("Whether **Reception** may cancel…"; `SEAT-PO-007` = `C`)* **are** the grant. ⭐ Consistent with `ADR-0153`, which held `ATT-CFG-022`/`024` on this exact ground |
| **⛔⛔ `SE-3` — `SEAT-CFG-017` HELD on three grounds** | ⛔ owner is **`BC-06` via `E-05`** — ⭐ the **only** one of 18 not owned by `BC-25` · ⛔ value *"**Unset in V1** — no event is emitted and **no value is substituted**"* · ⛔ **`SEAT-GAP-005` OPEN**. ⛔⛔ **The 90% figure was NOT applied as a fallback** — §27.1 forbids it in terms |
| **⛔ `SE-4` — real tenant `WRITE`, ⛔ not ⚪ `N/A`** | ⛔⛔ **`ADR-0151` §3.5 NOT reused** — its ⚪ `N/A` rests on `CNF-FR-020`, which binds only platform-default values. ⭐ `SEAT-PO-021` **positively grants** Owner a change operation, so a runtime write **demonstrably exists** — ⛔ `N/A` would be **false** |
| **⚠ `SEAT-XC-022` caveat honoured, ⛔ not flattened** | ⭐ *"tenant-scoped, **or branch-scoped where `BC-06` owns it**"* — ⭐⭐ its **sole referent is `SEAT-CFG-017`**, ⛔ **which is HELD**. ⭐ So all 15 allocated are `Tenant`, and ⛔ **no branch scope is converted to Tenant by inference** |
| **⭐ `NOT COMMERCIAL` is MANDATED, not merely satisfied** | ⭐ **`SEAT-FR-279`**: *"The module **MUST NOT** display or store **any monetary value**…"*; `SEAT-FR-144`, `SEAT-XC-011`, and the `BC-05` edge measured *"**None.** No edge exists"*. ⛔ A commercial `SEAT-CFG-*` would **breach a frozen `MUST NOT`** |
| **⛔ Supersedes** | ⛔⛔ **NOTHING** · ⚠ **`Count` cell at L9 NOT incremented** |
| **⛔ Preserves** | ⛔ **`PRD-007`** (`03a8ba0a…`, FROZEN v1.1) · ⛔ `ADR-0151`…`ADR-0156` · ⛔ **the 104 inventory** |
| **⛔ NOT done** | ⛔ **0** `TR-2`/`TR-3` read · ⛔ **0** `PR-n` authority · ⛔ **0** `TR-4`/`TR-5` authority · ⛔ `ICFG` not batched · ⛔ **0** `PERM-*` · **0** roles/actions/scopes · **0** frozen edits · **0** runtime code · ⚠⚠ **AUTHORIZED ≠ DESIGNED** |

## ⭐⭐ ADDENDUM — `ADR-0158` registration *(end-of-file; ⛔ L9 Count cell NOT edited)*

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **ADR** | ⭐⭐ **[`ADR-0158`](ADR-0158-security-owner-designation-and-phase-7-disposition-of-the-25-remaining-bc-25-parameters.md)** — *SECURITY PLATFORM designated "Security Owner" (security-review authority only), and the Phase 7 disposition of the 25 remaining parameters* |
| **Status** | ⭐⭐ **Accepted** — 2026-09-19 · **Rank 2** · `BC-25` *(owning)*; `BC-06`/`BC-13`/`BC-18` *(adjacent)* |
| **Authority** | ⭐⭐ **Seven decisions, verbatim** — `R-1` … `R-7` |
| **⛔⛔ IT AUTHORIZES NOTHING — DELIBERATELY** | ⭐ Coverage moves by **ZERO**, staying at **79 / 104**. ⛔⛔ **104/104 was NOT forced.** ⭐ What it achieves instead: it **constitutes an office that did not exist**, and **converts 25 unexplained gaps into 25 documented holds**, each with a reason and a named owner. ⭐⭐ **An audit that ends in HOLD is a result, not a failure** |
| **⭐ `R-1` — the office, and the gap it closes** | ⭐ `ADR-0153` routed 4 Face parameters to *"Product Owner + **Security Owner**"* and `ADR-0021` demands *"security input required"* — ⛔ **but `PRD_OWNERSHIP_MODEL` §2.2 constitutes only FOUR offices** *(Product, Domain, Architecture, Technical, **L83-86**)* and **`grep -c "security owner"` = 0**. ⭐ The named owner **did not exist**; now it does |
| **⛔⛔ THE LIMITS ARE LOAD-BEARING** | ⭐ **HAS:** security review · security decision. ⛔ **DOES NOT HAVE:** product · architecture · tenant · implementation · **permission** authority. ⚠⚠ **The permission exclusion means `R-1` does NOT unblock the 5 authorization-semantic parameters** — ⛔ **a security office is not an authorization office**, and this ADR does not merge them. ⭐ It is a **governance office**, ⛔ **NOT a runtime role**: `AUTH` ch.07 stays closed at **two** platform and **five** library roles |
| **⛔ `R-2` — 4 Face HELD, and the conditional was TESTED** | ⭐ `R-2` said *authorize **only if** explicit V1 evidence supports them*. ⭐ **Measured:** `013` *(Enabled)*, `015` *(Enabled)* and `016` *(Disabled)* **DO carry V1 defaults and domains** (§16.3a). ⛔⛔ **But a default value is not V1 authorization** — **`ATT-FR-064`**: *"Until `ATT-GAP-012` and `ATT-GAP-014` are **both** answered… **face verification MUST NOT be implemented**. The mode is specified; it is **not authorised to be built**"*, and **both gaps are OPEN**. ⭐ Allocating authority over settings of an **unbuildable mode would be authorization theatre**. ⛔ `014` is separately outside V1 *(`D-3a`, Face = **V3**; both columns read "Not in V1")* |
| **⛔ `R-3` — 5 authorization-semantic HELD** | ⛔ `ATT-CFG-022` *(§19.2 **L1980**)* · ⛔ `ATT-CFG-023` *(⭐ held **with** `022` — it time-bounds the very capability `022` gates; ⛔ deciding them apart could permit corrections **and** set the window to **0**)* · ⛔ `ATT-CFG-024` *(§19.2 **L1983**, also cross-app)* · ⛔ `SEAT-CFG-005`/`014` *(**`C`** cells). ⛔ **0** `PERM-*` created |
| **⛔⛔ `R-4` — 10 `ICFG` HELD, audited dimension by dimension** | ⭐ **Sufficient:** inventory *(10, contiguous, "Approved — authoritative")* · values *(all 10 defaults + ranges, `INV-13`…`INV-16`)* · commercial *(NOT COMMERCIAL)*. ⛔ **INSUFFICIENT:** **no `READ` statement anywhere** · **no `WRITE` role** *(only a prohibition — `INV-SEC-018`: "not overridable by **any library role, including `TR-1` Owner**")* · **ambiguous scope** *(`ICFG-5`/`6`/`7` say "per library" yet **L806** gives `ICFG-1` per-environment values, while the `LCFG-*` per-library carve-out at **L829** names **no** `ICFG-*`)* · **roles: `TR-1`×6, `TR-2`×6, `TR-3`×3, `PR-1`/`PR-2` = ZERO** |
| **⭐⭐ A `PR-n` `ICFG` ALLOCATION WAS CONSIDERED AND REFUSED** | ⚠ The tempting argument: the guide says *"**Every parameter in this register is security-owned**"*, `INV-SEC-018` denies **every library role**, and `ICFG-10`'s own rationale reads *"**Matches `CFG-4`**"* — a parameter already allocated to `PR-1`+`PR-2` by `ADR-0154`. ⛔⛔ **REFUSED on three grounds:** (1) ⭐ *"security-owned"* names a **reviewing office**, ⛔ not a runtime reader — and `R-1` **expressly denied that office permission authority** · (2) ⛔ **a denial of `TR-n` is not a grant to `PR-n`** *(`AUTH-7.28`, no hierarchy)* · (3) ⭐ *"Matches `CFG-4`"* is a **VALUE rationale, ⛔ NOT an authority statement** — ⚠⚠ **precisely the `SCFG` error refused at `ADR-0155`: a shared band, or here a shared value, is not a shared decision** |
| **⛔ `R-5`/`R-6`/`R-7` — 6 HELD** | ⛔ `CFG-10` + `SCFG-4` *(**Privacy Owner**, held together as one retention question)* · ⛔ `SMCFG-5` *(`SM-GAP-2`, **definition** undecided)* · ⛔ `SEAT-CFG-017` *(`BC-06`-owned, unset, `SEAT-GAP-005`)* · ⛔ `CFG-12` *(**self-referential** — bears on `PR-2`'s own standing)* · ⛔ `SCFG-2` *(reviewer **undefined**)* |
| **⭐ The 25, reconciled** | **4** Face + **5** authorization-semantic + **10** `ICFG` + **2** retention + **2** undefined + **2** authorization-other = ⭐ **25**. ⭐⭐ **79 authorized + 25 held = 104** — ⛔ no parameter unaccounted, ⛔ **none authorized to reach a number** |
| **⛔ Supersedes** | ⛔⛔ **NOTHING** · ⚠ **`Count` cell at L9 NOT incremented** |
| **⛔ Preserves** | ⛔ `PRD-006` · `PRD-007` · `INVITATION_SECURITY_SPECIFICATION` · `CONFIGURATION_GUIDE` · `PRD_OWNERSHIP_MODEL` *(⚠ recording the office **there** is `O-1`, a **Governance Owner** act)* · `ADR-0151`…`ADR-0157` · ⛔ **the 104 inventory** |
| **⛔ NOT done** | ⛔ **0** parameters authorized · ⛔ **0** `PERM-*` · **0** roles/actions/scopes · ⛔ `ATT-GAP-012`, `ATT-GAP-014`, `SM-GAP-2`, `SEAT-GAP-005`, `CNF-GAP-002`, `CNF-GAP-007` **all still OPEN** · **0** frozen edits · **0** runtime code |
| **Coverage** | ⭐ **UNCHANGED at 79 / 104** *(76.0%)*. ⛔⛔ **D3 stays `PARTIAL`; 25 remain HELD**; ⛔ **`DD-0007` is NOT design-ready** |

| **Status** | ⭐⭐ **Accepted** — 2026-09-19 |
| **Authority** | ⭐ **Two limbs, recorded separately rather than merged.** **Limb A** *(constitute)* — **Architecture Owner** (**L85**, **L284**) **+ Governance Owner** (**L418**), on the `ADR-0077` / `ADR-0080` precedent. **Limb B** *(record)* — **Governance Owner** alone, because `ADR-0158` §7 **`O-1`** names that office and no other. ⚠️ Both exercised by **one principal**, so ⛔ **no independent review, ARB quorum or Security audit is claimed** |
| ⭐⭐ **What it constitutes** | ⭐ **A tenth office — the `Authorization Owner` — as a STANDING office**, recorded in new `PRD_OWNERSHIP_MODEL` **§16**. ⛔ **Not** a one-act conferral (unlike §13/§14/§15): the rules it serves are continuing, and ⭐⭐ **three `Accepted` ADRs have ALREADY EXERCISED it** — `ADR-0150`, `ADR-0151`, `ADR-0153`, each recording *"The **Authorization Owner** conferral is recorded **in this ADR**"* |
| ⭐ **The derivation — 7 rules, none created here** | **Rank 5** `ARCHITECTURE_RULINGS.md` **L230** (*"**Authorization** \| Tenant roles \| `BC-18` — **authorization concern**"*) with **L232** (*"**Do not merge Authentication and Authorization responsibilities.**"*) · `ADR-0132` §7 rows **2, 3, 4, 5, 6** · `ADR-0150`/`0151`/`0153` **L8** · `PAYMENT_GOVERNANCE_RESOLUTION` **L104** (`SAAS-GAP-004`) · routing from `ADR-0155` `S-3`, `ADR-0157` `SE-2`, `ADR-0158` `R-3`/`R-7`/`O-3`/`O-7` |
| ⭐ **Measured before the act** | *"Authorization Owner"* — ⭐ **70** occurrences across **25** files, ⛔ **0** in `PRD_OWNERSHIP_MODEL`; **0** role-definition rows anywhere. ⭐ **The office was never missing from practice; only from the document whose purpose is to define offices.** §2.1's minimality clause is a **conditional test** and is **MET, not overridden**; §2.3 satisfied, and the anchored form *"Authorization (`BC-18`/`PRD-001`) Owner"* **preserved, not renamed** |
| ✅ **`O-1` DISCHARGED** | ⭐ The **`Security Owner`** office — designated by `ADR-0158` `R-1` — is **RECORDED** in new **§17**. ⭐⭐ **No new authority created:** the boundary is **transcribed** from `ADR-0158` §2.1b — ⭐ **HAS** security review + security decision; ⛔ **DOES NOT HAVE** product, architecture, tenant, implementation, **permission** or **permission-creation** authority |
| ⚠️⚠️ **One deviation, disclosed not glossed** | `O-1` says record it at **§2.2**; it is recorded at **§17**. ⛔ §2.2 spans **L81–L86** and **72** citations point at **L85** alone — an inserted row would misdirect them all, including the **16** to **L284** that are the evidence for the authority under which this ADR was conferred. ⭐ **Sixth office recorded outside §2.2 since `ADR-0077`** (§9, §10, §13, §14, §15). ⭐ `O-1`'s **substance** is satisfied in full; ⛔ **`ADR-0158` is NOT edited** to match |
| ⚠️⚠️ **The permission exclusion held** | ⛔⛔ Constituting the Authorization Owner ⛔ **does NOT relax** `ADR-0158`'s Security-Owner permission exclusion — ⭐ **it supplies the office the exclusion points at.** ⭐ **A security office is not an authorization office** |
| ⛔⛔ **What it AUTHORIZES** | ⛔⛔ **ZERO.** ⭐ Coverage **UNCHANGED at 79 / 104** *(76.0%)*; ⛔ **D3 stays `PARTIAL`**; ⛔ **`DD-0007` NOT design-ready**, still **v0.9**. ⛔ **All 25 `DP-0001` items remain HELD**; the pack stays **OPEN** with **0** authority responses; ⛔ **no disposition changed**. ⭐ **79 + 25 = 104** still reconciles exactly |
| ⭐⭐ **The discipline** | ⭐ **Constituting an office is not exercising it.** ⛔ `O-3` and `O-7` remain **OPEN** — the office that owns them now **exists**; the decisions do **not**. ⛔ Deciding a parameter in the act that creates its decider is the unbounded self-authorisation `ADR-0033` §7.1 warns against |

| ⛔ **Preserves** | ⛔ `ADR-0043` · `ADR-0060` *(SECURITY platform still has **no** bounded context)* · `ADR-0132` *(**not reopened**; ⛔ **none** of its §7 prerequisites engaged)* · `ADR-0151`…`ADR-0158` · `AUTH-7.22` *(catalogue still **zero**)* · `DP-0001` §5A · `DD-0007` · ⛔ **the 104 inventory** · every frozen PRD |
